# Conversation Summary

## 1. Previous Conversation

The conversation began with a request to initialize AGENTS.md files for the Meeting2Jira codebase. I analyzed the project structure and created comprehensive AGENTS.md files with project-specific, non-obvious information for AI coding assistants.

The user then requested help with testing Phase 2 implementation, which had been scaffolded using OpenAI. During this discussion, the user revealed that **OpenAI was incorrect** - the project should be using **IBM watsonx.ai** instead.

## 2. Current Work

### Initial Task: Create AGENTS.md Files
- Created main AGENTS.md in project root with build/test commands, code style guidelines, and project-specific patterns
- Created mode-specific AGENTS.md files in .bob/rules-code/, .bob/rules-advanced/, .bob/rules-ask/, and .bob/rules-plan/
- Focused on non-obvious information like mandatory file cleanup patterns, static class methods, and TypeScript strictness

### Testing Phase 2
Created comprehensive testing infrastructure:
- Unit tests for OpenAI service (later migrated to watsonx)
- Controller tests for extract endpoints
- Integration tests for complete API flow
- Jest configuration
- Test data samples and automated test scripts
- Testing documentation (TESTING_GUIDE.md, README_TESTING.md)

### Critical Migration: OpenAI → IBM watsonx.ai
User identified that Phase 2 was incorrectly implemented with OpenAI and needed to use IBM watsonx.ai instead. This required:

1. **Dependency Changes:**
   - Removed: `openai` package
   - Added: `@ibm-cloud/watsonx-ai` SDK

2. **Environment Variables:**
   - Old: `OPENAI_API_KEY`
   - New: `WATSONX_API_KEY`, `WATSONX_PROJECT_ID`, `WATSONX_URL`

3. **Service Implementation** (backend/src/services/watsonx.service.ts):
   - Correct import: `import { WatsonXAI } from '@ibm-cloud/watsonx-ai'`
   - Authentication: `IamAuthenticator` from `ibm-cloud-sdk-core`
   - Initialization: `WatsonXAI.newInstance()` method
   - API method: `textChat()` instead of `textGeneration()`
   - Parameters: `maxTokens`, `topP`, `topK`, `repetitionPenalty`
   - Response structure: `result.choices[0].message?.content`
   - Model: `ibm/granite-13b-chat-v2`

4. **Updated All Files:**
   - Controller (extract.controller.ts)
   - All test files (3 files)
   - All documentation (4 files)
   - Test scripts
   - Created WATSONX_MIGRATION.md guide

### .env File Explanation
User asked about .env file purpose and whether to commit it:
- **Purpose:** Stores sensitive configuration (API keys, passwords) that differ between environments
- **Should NOT be committed:** Contains secrets that cost money if stolen
- **What to commit:** `.env.example` (template with placeholders)
- **Already configured:** `.gitignore` prevents `.env` from being committed

### Technical Issues Resolved
Encountered TypeScript errors with watsonx.ai SDK:
- Initial error: Incorrect import syntax and constructor usage
- Fixed by: Using named import `{ WatsonXAI }`, `IamAuthenticator`, and `newInstance()` method
- Corrected API: Changed from `textGeneration()` to `textChat()` with proper parameter names

## 3. Key Technical Concepts

- **IBM watsonx.ai SDK:** Enterprise AI platform requiring API key + Project ID authentication
- **IamAuthenticator:** IBM Cloud authentication mechanism using API keys
- **Static Class Pattern:** Controllers and parsers use static methods (no instantiation)
- **Jest Testing:** Unit and integration tests with mocking
- **TypeScript Strict Mode:** All strict checks enabled including noUnusedLocals/Parameters
- **CommonJS Modules:** Backend uses CommonJS despite modern TypeScript
- **File Cleanup Pattern:** Mandatory `fs.unlink()` in both success and error paths
- **Environment Variables:** Sensitive configuration stored in .env (never committed)

## 4. Relevant Files and Code

### backend/src/services/watsonx.service.ts
- **Why important:** Core AI integration service for action item extraction
- **Changes made:** Complete rewrite from OpenAI to watsonx.ai
- **Key code:**
```typescript
import { WatsonXAI } from '@ibm-cloud/watsonx-ai';
import { IamAuthenticator } from 'ibm-cloud-sdk-core';

this.client = WatsonXAI.newInstance({
  version: '2024-05-31',
  serviceUrl: url,
  authenticator: new IamAuthenticator({ apikey: apiKey })
});

const response = await this.client.textChat({
  messages: [{ role: 'user', content: prompt }],
  modelId: 'ibm/granite-13b-chat-v2',
  projectId: this.projectId,
  maxTokens: 2000,
  temperature: 0.3
});
```

### backend/src/controllers/extract.controller.ts
- **Why important:** Handles API endpoints for extraction
- **Changes made:** Replaced OpenAIService with WatsonXService, updated error messages
- **Key pattern:** Static methods, lazy service initialization

### backend/.env.example
- **Why important:** Template for required environment variables
- **Changes made:** Replaced OPENAI_API_KEY with WATSONX_API_KEY, WATSONX_PROJECT_ID, WATSONX_URL

### Test Files (3 files)
- watsonx.service.test.ts: Tests service with mocked SDK
- extract.controller.test.ts: Tests controller endpoints
- integration.test.ts: End-to-end API tests
- **Changes made:** Updated all mocks and assertions for watsonx.ai

### Documentation Files (6 files)
- AGENTS.md: Project-specific guidance for AI assistants
- TESTING_GUIDE.md: Comprehensive testing documentation
- README_TESTING.md: Quick start testing guide
- PHASE2_COMPLETE.md: Implementation documentation
- WATSONX_MIGRATION.md: Migration guide from OpenAI
- ENV_SETUP_GUIDE.md: (mentioned but not created due to mode restrictions)

## 5. Problem Solving

### Problem 1: Incorrect AI Provider
- **Issue:** Phase 2 was scaffolded with OpenAI instead of IBM watsonx.ai
- **Solution:** Complete migration of service, tests, and documentation
- **Outcome:** Successfully integrated watsonx.ai with correct SDK usage

### Problem 2: TypeScript Compilation Errors
- **Issue:** `Cannot use namespace 'WatsonXAI' as a type`, `This expression is not constructable`
- **Root cause:** Incorrect import syntax and constructor usage
- **Solution:** Changed to named import `{ WatsonXAI }` and used `WatsonXAI.newInstance()` factory method
- **Outcome:** Code compiles successfully

### Problem 3: Wrong API Method
- **Issue:** `Property 'textGeneration' does not exist on type 'WatsonXAI'`
- **Root cause:** Used non-existent method name
- **Solution:** Changed to `textChat()` method with messages array format
- **Outcome:** Correct API usage matching SDK documentation

### Problem 4: Authentication Configuration
- **Issue:** `'apikey' does not exist in type 'AuthenticatorInterface'`
- **Root cause:** Direct object literal instead of proper authenticator class
- **Solution:** Used `IamAuthenticator` class from `ibm-cloud-sdk-core`
- **Outcome:** Proper IBM Cloud authentication

## 6. Pending Tasks and Next Steps

### Immediate Next Steps:
1. **Get watsonx.ai Credentials:**

   ```bash
   curl http://localhost:3000/api/extract/test
   ```

4. **Run Tests:**
   ```bash

   ```

## MAIN PAGE (after setup)
Two column layout, dark theme throughout:
Background: #0f1117
Card background: #1a1d27
Primary: #4F6EF7
Success: #22c55e  
Warning: #f59e0b
Border: #2d3149
Text: #ffffff, secondary: #94a3b8

NAVBAR:
- Left: green/red dot + "Meeting2Jira" 
- Right: small gear icon only (links to settings)
- No Upload/Review/Settings text links
- Dark #0a0d14 background, border-bottom #2d3149

LEFT COLUMN — Jira Config summary card:
- Shows "✓ Connected" state with domain and project
- Collapse/expand toggle to edit config
- Edit opens the form fields inline

RIGHT COLUMN — Upload card:
- Large drag-drop zone, dashed #2d3149 border
- Supported formats: .vtt .docx badges
- After file selected: show filename + size + 
  detected language with flag emoji
- "Extract Action Items" blue button
- Live processing steps shown as a vertical stepper:
  "📄 Parsing transcript..."
  "🤖 Analysing with IBM watsonx..."  
  "✅ Extraction complete! 7 items found"

## TOAST NOTIFICATIONS (top-right, not bottom-center)
Replace ALL existing snackbar/toast notifications:
- Position: fixed top-4 right-4 z-50
- Stack multiple toasts vertically downward
- Each toast: rounded-xl, shadow-lg, min-width 320px
- Success toast: dark card + green left border + 
  white checkmark circle + title + message + auto-dismiss 4s
- Error toast: dark card + red left border + 
  red X circle + title + message + manual dismiss
- Warning toast: dark card + amber left border
- Slide in from right animation on appear
- Slide out to right on dismiss
- Close X button top-right of each toast
- Show "SCRUM-52 created successfully → View in Jira" 
  with clickable link for ticket creation success

## REVIEW PAGE
- Same dark theme
- Top bar: "Review Action Items" h1 left + 
  "7 items found" badge + meeting filename shown
- Action bar below: Select All checkbox | 
  Export CSV | Export JSON | 
  "Create X Jira Tickets" blue button right-aligned
- 2 column responsive grid of ticket cards
- Each card: bg-[#1a1d27] rounded-2xl border border-[#2d3149]
  blue left border accent
- Card top row: priority badge + confidence % chip + delete btn
- CONFIDENCE SCORING:
  If confidence >= 80: green badge "High confidence"
  If confidence 70-79: blue badge "Good confidence"  
  If confidence < 70: amber dashed border on entire card +
    amber badge "⚠ Low confidence" +
    amber banner inside card: 
    "AI wasn't fully certain about this item. 
     Please verify before pushing to Jira."
- Editable fields: Title, Description (textarea), 
  Assignee, Priority dropdown, Due Date
- Source quote: italic, left blue border, smaller text
- After Jira creation: card gets green border + 
  "✓ SCRUM-52" badge + "View in Jira →" link opens new tab
- Empty state when no items: 
  centered icon + "No transcript uploaded yet" + 
  "Upload Transcript" button that navigates to upload

## ROUTING
- / → check config → redirect to /setup or /upload
- /setup → onboarding (only if not configured)
- /upload → main page
-Command 'review' (see below for command content) → review page
- Guard: if not configured and tries to go to /upload, 
  redirect to /setup

Keep all TypeScript service logic, HTTP calls, 
and data models exactly as they are.
Only modify: component templates, component styles, 
routing guards, and app-level styles.
Install and configure Tailwind CSS if not already set up.
</feedback>

<file><path>frontend/src/app/services/state.service.ts</path>
<content lines="1-102">
  1 | import { Injectable } from '@angular/core';
  2 | import { BehaviorSubject, Observable } from 'rxjs';
  3 | import { ActionItem, JiraConfig } from '../models/action-item.model';
  4 | import { ParsedTranscript } from './api.service';
  5 | 
  6 | @Injectable({
  7 |   providedIn: 'root'
  8 | })
  9 | export class StateService {
 10 |   private transcriptSubject = new BehaviorSubject<ParsedTranscript | null>(null);
 11 |   private actionItemsSubject = new BehaviorSubject<ActionItem[]>([]);
 12 |   private jiraConfigSubject = new BehaviorSubject<JiraConfig | null>(null);
 13 |   private loadingSubject = new BehaviorSubject<boolean>(false);
 14 | 
 15 |   transcript$: Observable<ParsedTranscript | null> = this.transcriptSubject.asObservable();
 16 |   actionItems$: Observable<ActionItem[]> = this.actionItemsSubject.asObservable();
 17 |   jiraConfig$: Observable<JiraConfig | null> = this.jiraConfigSubject.asObservable();
 18 |   loading$: Observable<boolean> = this.loadingSubject.asObservable();
 19 | 
 20 |   constructor() {
 21 |     // Load Jira config from localStorage on init
 22 |     this.loadJiraConfig();
 23 |   }
 24 | 
 25 |   setTranscript(transcript: ParsedTranscript | null): void {
 26 |     this.transcriptSubject.next(transcript);
 27 |   }
 28 | 
 29 |   getTranscript(): ParsedTranscript | null {
 30 |     return this.transcriptSubject.value;
 31 |   }
 32 | 
 33 |   setActionItems(items: ActionItem[]): void {
 34 |     this.actionItemsSubject.next(items);
 35 |   }
 36 | 
 37 |   getActionItems(): ActionItem[] {
 38 |     return this.actionItemsSubject.value;
 39 |   }
 40 | 
 41 |   addActionItem(item: ActionItem): void {
 42 |     const items = [...this.actionItemsSubject.value, item];
 43 |     this.actionItemsSubject.next(items);
 44 |   }
 45 | 
 46 |   updateActionItem(id: string, updates: Partial<ActionItem>): void {
 47 |     const items = this.actionItemsSubject.value.map(item =>
 48 |       item.id === id ? { ...item, ...updates } : item
 49 |     );
 50 |     this.actionItemsSubject.next(items);
 51 |   }
 52 | 
 53 |   removeActionItem(id: string): void {
 54 |     const items = this.actionItemsSubject.value.filter(item => item.id !== id);
 55 |     this.actionItemsSubject.next(items);
 56 |   }
 57 | 
 58 |   clearActionItems(): void {
 59 |     this.actionItemsSubject.next([]);
 60 |   }
 61 | 
 62 |   setJiraConfig(config: JiraConfig | null): void {
 63 |     this.jiraConfigSubject.next(config);
 64 |     if (config) {
 65 |       localStorage.setItem('jiraConfig', JSON.stringify(config));
 66 |     } else {
 67 |       localStorage.removeItem('jiraConfig');
 68 |     }
 69 |   }
 70 | 
 71 |   getJiraConfig(): JiraConfig | null {
 72 |     return this.jiraConfigSubject.value;
 73 |   }
 74 | 
 75 |   private loadJiraConfig(): void {
 76 |     const stored = localStorage.getItem('jiraConfig');
 77 |     if (stored) {
 78 |       try {
 79 |         const config = JSON.parse(stored);
 80 |         this.jiraConfigSubject.next(config);
 81 |       } catch (e) {
 82 |         console.error('Failed to parse stored Jira config', e);
 83 |       }
 84 |     }
 85 |   }
 86 | 
 87 |   setLoading(loading: boolean): void {
 88 |     this.loadingSubject.next(loading);
 89 |   }
 90 | 
 91 |   isLoading(): boolean {
 92 |     return this.loadingSubject.value;
 93 |   }
 94 | 
 95 |   reset(): void {
 96 |     this.transcriptSubject.next(null);
 97 |     this.actionItemsSubject.next([]);
 98 |     this.loadingSubject.next(false);
 99 |   }
100 | }
101 | 
102 | // Made with Bob
</content>

<path>frontend/src/app/services/toast.service.ts</path>
<content>import { Injectable } from '@angular/core';
import { BehaviorSubject } from 'rxjs';

export interface Toast {
  id: string;
  type: 'success' | 'error' | 'warning' | 'info';
  title: string;
  message: string;
  link?: {
    text: string;
    url: string;
  };
  autoDismiss?: boolean;
  duration?: number;
}

@Injectable({
  providedIn: 'root'
})
export class ToastService {
  private toastsSubject = new BehaviorSubject<Toast[]>([]);
  toasts$ = this.toastsSubject.asObservable();

  show(toast: Omit<Toast, 'id'>): void {
    const id = `toast-${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}`;
    const newToast: Toast = {
      id,
      autoDismiss: toast.type === 'success' ? true : false,
      duration: 4000,
      ...toast
    };

    const toasts = [...this.toastsSubject.value, newToast];
    this.toastsSubject.next(toasts);

    if (newToast.autoDismiss) {
      setTimeout(() => this.dismiss(id), newToast.duration);
    }
  }

  success(title: string, message: string, link?: { text: string; url: string }): void {
    this.show({ type: 'success', title, message, link });
  }

  error(title: string, message: string): void {
    this.show({ type: 'error', title, message });
  }

  warning(title: string, message: string): void {
    this.show({ type: 'warning', title, message });
  }

  info(title: string, message: string): void {
    this.show({ type: 'info', title, message });
  }

  dismiss(id: string): void {
    const toasts = this.toastsSubject.value.filter(t => t.id !== id);
    this.toastsSubject.next(toasts);
  }

  clear(): void {
    this.toastsSubject.next([]);
  }
}

// Made with Bob
</content>
<line_count>68</line_count>

<content>import { Component } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { ToastService, Toast } from '../../services/toast.service';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-toast',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule],
  template: `
    <div class="fixed top-4 right-4 z-50 flex flex-col gap-3 max-w-md">
      <div
        *ngFor="let toast of toasts$ | async"
        class="bg-dark-card rounded-xl shadow-lg min-w-[320px] overflow-hidden animate-slide-in"
        [class.border-l-4]="true"
        [class.border-success]="toast.type === 'success'"
        [class.border-red-500]="toast.type === 'error'"
        [class.border-warning]="toast.type === 'warning'"
        [class.border-blue-500]="toast.type === 'info'"
      >
        <div class="p-4 flex items-start gap-3">
          <!-- Icon -->
          <div
            class="flex-shrink-0 w-8 h-8 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center"
            [class.bg-success]="toast.type === 'success'"
            [class.bg-red-500]="toast.type === 'error'"
            [class.bg-warning]="toast.type === 'warning'"
            [class.bg-blue-500]="toast.type === 'info'"
          >
            <svg *ngIf="toast.type === 'success'" class="w-5 h-5 text-white" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
              <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7"></path>
            </svg>
            <svg *ngIf="toast.type === 'error'" class="w-5 h-5 text-white" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
              <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12"></path>
            </svg>
            <svg *ngIf="toast.type === 'warning'" class="w-5 h-5 text-white" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
              <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M12 9v2m0 4h.01m-6.938 4h13.856c1.54 0 2.502-1.667 1.732-3L13.732 4c-.77-1.333-2.694-1.333-3.464 0L3.34 16c-.77 1.333.192 3 1.732 3z"></path>
            </svg>
            <svg *ngIf="toast.type === 'info'" class="w-5 h-5 text-white" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
              <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M13 16h-1v-4h-1m1-4h.01M21 12a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z"></path>
            </svg>
          </div>

          <!-- Content -->
          <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">
            <h4 class="text-white font-semibold text-sm mb-1">{{ toast.title }}</h4>
            <p class="text-gray-400 text-sm">{{ toast.message }}</p>
            <a
              *ngIf="toast.link"
              [href]="toast.link.url"
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              class="text-primary hover:text-blue-400 text-sm font-medium inline-flex items-center gap-1 mt-2"
            >
              {{ toast.link.text }}
              <svg class="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M10 6H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v10a2 2 0 002 2h10a2 2 0 002-2v-4M14 4h6m0 0v6m0-6L10 14"></path>
              </svg>
            </a>
          </div>

          <!-- Close button -->
          <button
            (click)="dismiss(toast.id)"
            class="flex-shrink-0 text-gray-400 hover:text-white transition-colors"
          >
            <svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
              <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12"></path>
            </svg>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  `,
  styles: [`
    @keyframes slide-in {
      from {
        transform: translateX(100%);
        opacity: 0;
      }
      to {
        transform: translateX(0);
        opacity: 1;
      }
    }

    .animate-slide-in {
      animation: slide-in 0.3s ease-out;
    }
  `]
})
export class ToastComponent {
  toasts$ = this.toastService.toasts$;

  constructor(private toastService: ToastService) {}

  dismiss(id: string): void {
    this.toastService.dismiss(id);
  }
}

// Made with Bob
</content>

<path>frontend/src/app/guards/config.guard.ts</path>
<content>import { inject } from '@angular/core';
import { Router, CanActivateFn } from '@angular/router';
import { StateService } from '../services/state.service';

export const configGuard: CanActivateFn = () => {
  const stateService = inject(StateService);
  const router = inject(Router);

  const config = stateService.getJiraConfig();
  
  if (!config) {
    // No config found, redirect to setup
    return router.createUrlTree(['/setup']);
  }
  
  return true;
};

export const setupGuard: CanActivateFn = () => {
  const stateService = inject(StateService);
  const router = inject(Router);

  const config = stateService.getJiraConfig();
  
  if (config) {
    // Already configured, redirect to upload
    return router.createUrlTree(['/upload']);
  }
  
  return true;
};

// Made with Bob
</content>

import { Component, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { RouterOutlet, RouterLink } from '@angular/router';
import { StateService } from './services/state.service';
import { ToastComponent } from './components/toast/toast.component';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-root',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, RouterOutlet, RouterLink, ToastComponent],
  templateUrl: './app.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./app.component.css']
})
export class AppComponent implements OnInit {
  isConnected = false;

  constructor(private stateService: StateService) {}

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.stateService.jiraConfig$.subscribe(config => {
      this.isConnected = !!config;
    });
  }
}

 33 | export class SettingsComponent implements OnInit {
 34 |   config: JiraConfig = {
 35 |     domain: '',
 36 |     email: '',
 37 |     apiToken: '',
 38 |     projectKey: ''
 39 |   };
 40 | 
 41 |   isTesting = false;
 42 |   isConfigured = false;
 43 | 
 44 |   constructor(
 45 |     private stateService: StateService,
 46 |     private apiService: ApiService,
 47 |     private snackBar: MatSnackBar
 48 |   ) {}
 49 | 
 50 |   ngOnInit(): void {
 51 |     const savedConfig = this.stateService.getJiraConfig();
 52 |     if (savedConfig) {
 53 |       this.config = { ...savedConfig };
 54 |       this.isConfigured = true;
 55 |     }
 56 |   }
 57 | 
 58 |   testConnection(): void {
 59 |     if (!this.isFormValid()) {
 60 |       this.snackBar.open('Please fill in all fields', 'Close', { duration: 3000 });
 61 |       return;
 62 |     }
 63 | 
 64 |     this.isTesting = true;
 65 | 
 66 |     this.apiService.testJiraConnection(this.config).subscribe({
 67 |       next: (response) => {
 68 |         this.isTesting = false;
 69 |         
 70 |         if (response.success) {
 71 |           this.snackBar.open('Connection successful!', 'Close', { duration: 3000 });
 72 |         } else {
 73 |           this.snackBar.open(
 74 |             response.data?.message || response.error || 'Connection failed',
 75 |             'Close',
 76 |             { duration: 5000 }
 77 |           );
 78 |         }
 79 |       },
 80 |       error: (error) => {
 81 |         this.isTesting = false;
 82 |         this.snackBar.open('Connection failed: ' + error.message, 'Close', { duration: 5000 });
 83 |       }
 84 |     });
 85 |   }
 86 | 
 87 |   saveConfiguration(): void {
 88 |     if (!this.isFormValid()) {
 89 |       this.snackBar.open('Please fill in all fields', 'Close', { duration: 3000 });
 90 |       return;
 91 |     }
 92 | 
 93 |     this.stateService.setJiraConfig(this.config);
 94 |     this.isConfigured = true;
 95 |     this.snackBar.open('Configuration saved successfully!', 'Close', { duration: 3000 });
 96 |   }
 97 | 
 98 |   clearConfiguration(): void {
 99 |     this.config = {
100 |       domain: '',
101 |       email: '',
102 |       apiToken: '',
103 |       projectKey: ''
104 |     };
105 |     this.stateService.setJiraConfig(null);
106 |     this.isConfigured = false;
107 |     this.snackBar.open('Configuration cleared', 'Close', { duration: 3000 });
108 |   }
109 | 
110 |   isFormValid(): boolean {
111 |     return !!(
112 |       this.config.domain &&
113 |       this.config.email &&
114 |       this.config.apiToken &&
115 |       this.config.projectKey
116 |     );
117 |   }
118 | }

66 |       `${this.apiUrl}/api/jira/test`,
67 |       config
68 |     );

<content>import { Component, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';
import { Router } from '@angular/router';

import { JiraConfig } from '../../models/action-item.model';
import { StateService } from '../../services/state.service';
import { ApiService } from '../../services/api.service';
import { ToastService } from '../../services/toast.service';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-settings',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, FormsModule],
  templateUrl: './settings.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./settings.component.css']
})
export class SettingsComponent implements OnInit {
  config: JiraConfig = {
    domain: '',
    email: '',
    apiToken: '',
    projectKey: ''
  };

  isTesting = false;
  testSuccess = false;
  testError = '';
  isFirstTime = false;

  constructor(
    private stateService: StateService,
    private apiService: ApiService,
    private toastService: ToastService,
    private router: Router
  ) {}

  ngOnInit(): void {
    const savedConfig = this.stateService.getJiraConfig();
    if (savedConfig) {
      this.config = { ...savedConfig };
      this.isFirstTime = false;
    } else {
      this.isFirstTime = true;
    }
  }

  testConnection(): void {
    if (!this.isFormValid()) {
      this.toastService.error('Validation Error', 'Please fill in all fields');
      return;
    }

    this.isTesting = true;
    this.testSuccess = false;
    this.testError = '';

    this.apiService.testJiraConnection(this.config).subscribe({
      next: (response) => {
        this.isTesting = false;
        
        if (response.success) {
          this.testSuccess = true;
          this.toastService.success(
            'Connection Successful',
            `Connected to ${this.config.projectKey} project`
          );
        } else {
          this.testError = response.data?.message || response.error || 'Connection failed';
          this.toastService.error('Connection Failed', this.testError);
        }
      },
      error: (error) => {
        this.isTesting = false;
        this.testError = error.message || 'Connection failed';
        this.toastService.error('Connection Failed', this.testError);
      }
    });
  }

  continueToApp(): void {
    if (!this.testSuccess) {
      this.toastService.warning('Test Required', 'Please test the connection first');
      return;
    }

    this.stateService.setJiraConfig(this.config);
    this.toastService.success('Configuration Saved', 'Redirecting to app...');
    
    setTimeout(() => {
      this.router.navigate(['/upload']);
    }, 1000);
  }

  saveConfiguration(): void {
    if (!this.isFormValid()) {
      this.toastService.error('Validation Error', 'Please fill in all fields');
      return;
    }

    this.stateService.setJiraConfig(this.config);
    this.toastService.success('Configuration Saved', 'Your Jira settings have been updated');
  }

  isFormValid(): boolean {
    return !!(
      this.config.domain &&
      this.config.email &&
      this.config.apiToken &&
      this.config.projectKey
    );
  }
}

// Made with Bob
</content>

<path>frontend/src/app/components/settings/settings.component.html</path>
<content><div class="min-h-screen bg-dark-bg flex items-center justify-center p-6">
  <div class="w-full max-w-2xl">
    <!-- Logo and Title -->
    <div class="text-center mb-8">
      <h1 class="text-4xl font-bold text-white mb-2">Meeting2Jira</h1>
      <p class="text-gray-400 text-lg">Turn meeting transcripts into Jira tickets instantly</p>
    </div>

    <!-- Main Card -->
    <div class="bg-dark-card rounded-2xl border border-dark-border p-8 shadow-xl">
      <!-- Step Indicator -->
      <div class="mb-6">
        <div class="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-gray-400">
          <span class="bg-primary text-white rounded-full w-6 h-6 flex items-center justify-center text-xs font-semibold">1</span>
          <span>Step 1 of 1 — Connect your Jira</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Form -->
      <form class="space-y-5">
        <!-- Jira Domain -->
        <div>
          <label class="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-300 mb-2">Jira Domain</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            [(ngModel)]="config.domain"
            name="domain"
            placeholder="yourcompany.atlassian.net"
            class="w-full bg-dark-bg border border-dark-border rounded-lg px-4 py-3 text-white placeholder-gray-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary focus:border-transparent"
          />
          <p class="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1">Your Jira cloud domain (without https://)</p>
        </div>

        <!-- Email -->
        <div>
          <label class="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-300 mb-2">Email</label>
          <input
            type="email"
            [(ngModel)]="config.email"
            name="email"
            placeholder="your.email@company.com"
            class="w-full bg-dark-bg border border-dark-border rounded-lg px-4 py-3 text-white placeholder-gray-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary focus:border-transparent"
          />
          <p class="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1">Your Jira account email</p>
        </div>

        <!-- API Token -->
        <div>
          <label class="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-300 mb-2">API Token</label>
          <input
            type="password"
            [(ngModel)]="config.apiToken"
            name="apiToken"
            placeholder="Your Jira API token"
            class="w-full bg-dark-bg border border-dark-border rounded-lg px-4 py-3 text-white placeholder-gray-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary focus:border-transparent"
          />
          <p class="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1">
            <a href="https://id.atlassian.com/manage-profile/security/api-tokens" target="_blank" class="text-primary hover:text-blue-400">
              Generate token
            </a>
          </p>
        </div>

        <!-- Project Key -->
        <div>
          <label class="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-300 mb-2">Project Key</label>
          <input
            type="text"
            [(ngModel)]="config.projectKey"
            name="projectKey"
            placeholder="SCRUM"
            class="w-full bg-dark-bg border border-dark-border rounded-lg px-4 py-3 text-white placeholder-gray-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary focus:border-transparent"
          />
          <p class="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1">The key of your Jira project (e.g., SCRUM, DEV)</p>
        </div>
      </form>

      <!-- Test Connection Button -->
      <button
        (click)="testConnection()"
        [disabled]="!isFormValid() || isTesting"
        class="w-full mt-6 bg-primary hover:bg-blue-600 disabled:bg-gray-600 disabled:cursor-not-allowed text-white font-semibold py-3 px-6 rounded-lg transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
      >
        <svg *ngIf="isTesting" class="animate-spin h-5 w-5" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
          <circle class="opacity-25" cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="4"></circle>
          <path class="opacity-75" fill="currentColor" d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z"></path>
        </svg>
        <span>{{ isTesting ? 'Testing Connection...' : 'Test Connection' }}</span>
      </button>

      <!-- Success Message -->
      <div *ngIf="testSuccess" class="mt-4 bg-success/10 border border-success rounded-lg p-4 flex items-start gap-3">
        <svg class="w-6 h-6 text-success flex-shrink-0" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
          <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M9 12l2 2 4-4m6 2a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z"></path>
        </svg>
        <div>
          <p class="text-success font-semibold">Connected to {{ config.projectKey }} project</p>
          <p class="text-sm text-gray-400 mt-1">Your Jira connection is working correctly</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Error Message -->
      <div *ngIf="testError" class="mt-4 bg-red-500/10 border border-red-500 rounded-lg p-4 flex items-start gap-3">
        <svg class="w-6 h-6 text-red-500 flex-shrink-0" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
          <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M12 8v4m0 4h.01M21 12a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z"></path>
        </svg>
        <div>
          <p class="text-red-500 font-semibold">Connection Failed</p>
          <p class="text-sm text-gray-400 mt-1">{{ testError }}</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Continue Button (First Time) -->
      <button
        *ngIf="isFirstTime && testSuccess"
        (click)="continueToApp()"
        class="w-full mt-4 bg-success hover:bg-green-600 text-white font-semibold py-3 px-6 rounded-lg transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
      >
        <span>Continue to App</span>
        <svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
          <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M13 7l5 5m0 0l-5 5m5-5H6"></path>
        </svg>
      </button>

      <!-- Save Button (Returning User) -->
      <button
        *ngIf="!isFirstTime"
        (click)="saveConfiguration()"
        [disabled]="!isFormValid()"
        class="w-full mt-4 bg-success hover:bg-green-600 disabled:bg-gray-600 disabled:cursor-not-allowed text-white font-semibold py-3 px-6 rounded-lg transition-colors"
      >
        Save Configuration
      </button>
    </div>

    <!-- Security Notice -->
    <div class="mt-6 text-center text-sm text-gray-500">
      <svg class="w-4 h-4 inline-block mr-1" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
        <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M12 15v2m-6 4h12a2 2 0 002-2v-6a2 2 0 00-2-2H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v6a2 2 0 002 2zm10-10V7a4 4 0 00-8 0v4h8z"></path>
      </svg>
      Your credentials are stored locally and never sent to our servers
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- Made with Bob -->
</content>
<line_count>153</line_count>

<content>import { Component, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { Router } from '@angular/router';
import { HttpEventType } from '@angular/common/http';

import { ApiService, ParsedTranscript } from '../../services/api.service';
import { StateService } from '../../services/state.service';
import { ToastService } from '../../services/toast.service';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-upload',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule],
  templateUrl: './upload.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./upload.component.css']
})
export class UploadComponent implements OnInit {
  isDragging = false;
  uploadProgress = 0;
  isUploading = false;
  isExtracting = false;
  selectedFile: File | null = null;
  transcript: ParsedTranscript | null = null;
  supportedExtensions: string[] = ['.vtt', '.docx'];
  
  // Processing steps
  processingSteps = {
    parsing: false,
    analyzing: false,
    complete: false
  };
  extractedCount = 0;

  constructor(
    private apiService: ApiService,
    private stateService: StateService,
    private router: Router,
    private toastService: ToastService
  ) {}

  ngOnInit(): void {
    // Load supported file types from backend
    this.apiService.getSupportedTypes().subscribe({
      next: (response) => {
        if (response.success && response.data) {
          this.supportedExtensions = response.data.types;
        }
      },
      error: (error) => {
        console.error('Failed to load supported types', error);
      }
    });
  }

  get jiraConfig() {
    return this.stateService.getJiraConfig();
  }

  get detectedLanguage(): string {
    // Simple language detection based on file name or content
    // For now, return a default
    return 'English';
  }

  onDragOver(event: DragEvent): void {
    event.preventDefault();
    event.stopPropagation();
    this.isDragging = true;
  }

  onDragLeave(event: DragEvent): void {
    event.preventDefault();
    event.stopPropagation();
    this.isDragging = false;
  }

  onDrop(event: DragEvent): void {
    event.preventDefault();
    event.stopPropagation();
    this.isDragging = false;

    const files = event.dataTransfer?.files;
    if (files && files.length > 0) {
      this.handleFile(files[0]);
    }
  }

  onFileSelected(event: Event): void {
    const input = event.target as HTMLInputElement;
    if (input.files && input.files.length > 0) {
      this.handleFile(input.files[0]);
    }
  }

  handleFile(file: File): void {
    // Validate file type
    const extension = '.' + file.name.split('.').pop()?.toLowerCase();
    if (!this.supportedExtensions.includes(extension)) {
      this.toastService.error(
        'Invalid File Type',
        `Supported formats: ${this.supportedExtensions.join(', ')}`
      );
      return;
    }

    this.selectedFile = file;
    this.uploadFile();
  }

  uploadFile(): void {
    if (!this.selectedFile) return;

    this.isUploading = true;
    this.uploadProgress = 0;
    this.transcript = null;
    this.processingSteps = { parsing: false, analyzing: false, complete: false };

    this.apiService.uploadTranscript(this.selectedFile).subscribe({
      next: (event) => {
        if (event.type === HttpEventType.UploadProgress) {
          if (event.total) {
            this.uploadProgress = Math.round((100 * event.loaded) / event.total);
          }
        } else if (event.type === HttpEventType.Response) {
          this.isUploading = false;
          if (event.body?.success && event.body.data) {
            this.transcript = event.body.data;
            this.stateService.setTranscript(this.transcript);
            this.toastService.success('Upload Complete', 'Transcript uploaded successfully');
          } else {
            this.toastService.error('Upload Failed', event.body?.error || 'Unknown error');
          }
        }
      },
      error: (error) => {
        this.isUploading = false;
        this.toastService.error('Upload Failed', error.message);
      }
    });
  }

  extractActionItems(): void {
    if (!this.transcript) return;

    this.isExtracting = true;
    this.processingSteps.parsing = true;
    this.stateService.setLoading(true);

    // Simulate parsing step
    setTimeout(() => {
      this.processingSteps.analyzing = true;
    }, 1000);

    this.apiService.extractActionItems(this.transcript).subscribe({
      next: (response) => {
        this.isExtracting = false;
        this.stateService.setLoading(false);

        if (response.success && response.data) {
          this.processingSteps.complete = true;
          this.extractedCount = response.data.totalCount;
          
          setTimeout(() => {
            this.stateService.setActionItems(response.data.items);
            this.toastService.success(
              'Extraction Complete',
              `${response.data.totalCount} action items found`
            );
            this.router.navigate(['/review']);
          }, 1000);
        } else {
          this.processingSteps = { parsing: false, analyzing: false, complete: false };
          this.toastService.error('Extraction Failed', response.error || 'Unknown error');
        }
      },
      error: (error) => {
        this.isExtracting = false;
        this.stateService.setLoading(false);
        this.processingSteps = { parsing: false, analyzing: false, complete: false };
        this.toastService.error('Extraction Failed', error.message);
      }
    });
  }

  reset(): void {
    this.selectedFile = null;
    this.transcript = null;
    this.uploadProgress = 0;
    this.processingSteps = { parsing: false, analyzing: false, complete: false };
    this.extractedCount = 0;
    this.stateService.reset();
  }

  formatFileSize(bytes: number): string {
    if (bytes < 1024) return bytes + ' B';
    if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) return (bytes / 1024).toFixed(2) + ' KB';
    return (bytes / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(2) + ' MB';
  }
}

// Made with Bob
</content>

<content><div class="min-h-screen bg-dark-bg p-6">
  <div class="max-w-7xl mx-auto">
    <div class="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
      
      <!-- LEFT COLUMN - Jira Config Summary -->
      <div class="lg:col-span-1">
        <div class="bg-dark-card rounded-2xl border border-dark-border p-6">
          <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
            <h2 class="text-lg font-semibold text-white">Jira Connection</h2>
            <a routerLink="/setup" class="text-primary hover:text-blue-400 text-sm">Edit</a>
          </div>
          
          <div *ngIf="jiraConfig" class="space-y-3">
            <div class="flex items-center gap-2 text-success">
              <svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M9 12l2 2 4-4m6 2a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z"></path>
              </svg>
              <span class="font-medium">Connected</span>
            </div>
            
            <div class="space-y-2 text-sm">
              <div>
                <span class="text-gray-400">Domain:</span>
                <p class="text-white">{{ jiraConfig.domain }}</p>
              </div>
              <div>
                <span class="text-gray-400">Project:</span>
                <p class="text-white font-semibold">{{ jiraConfig.projectKey }}</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          
          <div *ngIf="!jiraConfig" class="text-center py-4">
            <p class="text-gray-400 text-sm mb-3">Not configured</p>
            <a routerLink="/setup" class="text-primary hover:text-blue-400 text-sm font-medium">
              Configure Jira →
            </a>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- RIGHT COLUMN - Upload Card -->
      <div class="lg:col-span-2">
        <div class="bg-dark-card rounded-2xl border border-dark-border p-8">
          <h2 class="text-2xl font-bold text-white mb-6">Upload Transcript</h2>
          
          <!-- Drag and Drop Zone -->
          <div
            class="border-2 border-dashed rounded-xl p-12 text-center transition-all cursor-pointer"
            [class.border-primary]="isDragging"
            [class.bg-primary/5]="isDragging"
            [class.border-dark-border]="!isDragging && !selectedFile"
            [class.border-success]="selectedFile && !isDragging"
            [class.bg-success/5]="selectedFile && !isDragging"
            (dragover)="onDragOver($event)"
            (dragleave)="onDragLeave($event)"
            (drop)="onDrop($event)"
            (click)="fileInput.click()"
          >
            <svg *ngIf="!selectedFile" class="w-16 h-16 mx-auto mb-4 text-gray-500" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
              <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M7 16a4 4 0 01-.88-7.903A5 5 0 1115.9 6L16 6a5 5 0 011 9.9M15 13l-3-3m0 0l-3 3m3-3v12"></path>
            </svg>
            
            <svg *ngIf="selectedFile" class="w-16 h-16 mx-auto mb-4 text-success" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
              <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M9 12h6m-6 4h6m2 5H7a2 2 0 01-2-2V5a2 2 0 012-2h5.586a1 1 0 01.707.293l5.414 5.414a1 1 0 01.293.707V19a2 2 0 01-2 2z"></path>
            </svg>

            <div *ngIf="!selectedFile">
              <p class="text-xl text-white mb-2">Drag and drop your transcript here</p>
              <p class="text-gray-400 mb-4">or click to browse</p>
              <div class="flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                <span class="px-3 py-1 bg-dark-bg rounded-full text-xs text-gray-400">.vtt</span>
                <span class="px-3 py-1 bg-dark-bg rounded-full text-xs text-gray-400">.docx</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div *ngIf="selectedFile">
              <p class="text-xl text-white font-semibold mb-2">{{ selectedFile.name }}</p>
              <p class="text-gray-400 mb-2">{{ formatFileSize(selectedFile.size) }}</p>
              <div class="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-sm">
                <span class="text-gray-400">Detected language:</span>
                <span class="text-white">🇺🇸 {{ detectedLanguage }}</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            <input
              #fileInput
              type="file"
              [accept]="supportedExtensions.join(',')"
              (change)="onFileSelected($event)"
              class="hidden"
            />
          </div>

          <!-- Upload Progress -->
          <div *ngIf="isUploading" class="mt-6">
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
              <span class="text-sm text-gray-400">Uploading...</span>
              <span class="text-sm text-white font-semibold">{{ uploadProgress }}%</span>
            </div>
            <div class="w-full bg-dark-bg rounded-full h-2">
              <div class="bg-primary h-2 rounded-full transition-all" [style.width.%]="uploadProgress"></div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- Extract Button -->
          <button
            *ngIf="transcript && !isExtracting"
            (click)="extractActionItems()"
            class="w-full mt-6 bg-primary hover:bg-blue-600 text-white font-semibold py-4 px-6 rounded-lg transition-colors"
          >
            Extract Action Items
          </button>

          <!-- Processing Steps -->
          <div *ngIf="isExtracting" class="mt-8 space-y-4">
            <div class="flex items-start gap-3">
              <div class="flex-shrink-0 mt-1">
                <svg *ngIf="!processingSteps.parsing" class="animate-spin h-5 w-5 text-primary" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                  <circle class="opacity-25" cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="4"></circle>
                  <path class="opacity-75" fill="currentColor" d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z"></path>
                </svg>
                <svg *ngIf="processingSteps.parsing" class="h-5 w-5 text-success" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                  <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7"></path>
                </svg>
              </div>
              <div>
                <p class="text-white font-medium">📄 Parsing transcript...</p>
                <p class="text-sm text-gray-400">Reading and structuring the content</p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div class="flex items-start gap-3" [class.opacity-50]="!processingSteps.parsing">
              <div class="flex-shrink-0 mt-1">
                <svg *ngIf="processingSteps.parsing && !processingSteps.analyzing" class="animate-spin h-5 w-5 text-primary" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                  <circle class="opacity-25" cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="4"></circle>
                  <path class="opacity-75" fill="currentColor" d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z"></path>
                </svg>
                <svg *ngIf="processingSteps.analyzing" class="h-5 w-5 text-success" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                  <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7"></path>
                </svg>
                <div *ngIf="!processingSteps.parsing" class="h-5 w-5 rounded-full border-2 border-gray-600"></div>
              </div>
              <div>
                <p class="text-white font-medium">🤖 Analyzing with IBM watsonx...</p>
                <p class="text-sm text-gray-400">Extracting action items using AI</p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div class="flex items-start gap-3" [class.opacity-50]="!processingSteps.analyzing">
              <div class="flex-shrink-0 mt-1">
                <svg *ngIf="processingSteps.analyzing && !processingSteps.complete" class="animate-spin h-5 w-5 text-primary" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                  <circle class="opacity-25" cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="4"></circle>
                  <path class="opacity-75" fill="currentColor" d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z"></path>
                </svg>
                <svg *ngIf="processingSteps.complete" class="h-5 w-5 text-success" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                  <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7"></path>
                </svg>
                <div *ngIf="!processingSteps.analyzing" class="h-5 w-5 rounded-full border-2 border-gray-600"></div>
              </div>
              <div>
                <p class="text-white font-medium">✅ Extraction complete! {{ extractedCount }} items found</p>
                <p class="text-sm text-gray-400">Ready for review</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- Reset Button -->
          <button
            *ngIf="selectedFile && !isUploading && !isExtracting"
            (click)="reset()"
            class="w-full mt-4 border border-dark-border hover:border-gray-500 text-gray-400 hover:text-white font-medium py-3 px-6 rounded-lg transition-colors"
          >
            Reset
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- Made with Bob -->
</content>
<line_count>197</line_count>
</write_to_file>

  8 | import { MatFormFieldModule } from '@angular/material/form-field';
  9 | import { MatInputModule } from '@angular/material/input';
 10 | import { MatSelectModule } from '@angular/material/select';
 11 | import { MatDatepickerModule } from '@angular/material/datepicker';
 12 | import { MatNativeDateModule } from '@angular/material/core';
 13 | import { MatChipsModule } from '@angular/material/chips';
 14 | import { MatCheckboxModule } from '@angular/material/checkbox';
 15 | import { MatSnackBar, MatSnackBarModule } from '@angular/material/snack-bar';

 24 |   selector: 'app-review',
 25 |   standalone: true,
 26 |   imports: [
 27 |     CommonModule,
 28 |     FormsModule,
 29 |     MatCardModule,
 30 |     MatButtonModule,
 31 |     MatIconModule,
 32 |     MatFormFieldModule,
 33 |     MatInputModule,
 34 |     MatSelectModule,
 35 |     MatDatepickerModule,
 36 |     MatNativeDateModule,
 37 |     MatChipsModule,
 38 |     MatCheckboxModule,
 39 |     MatSnackBarModule,
 40 |     MatProgressSpinnerModule,
 41 |     MatDialogModule
 42 |   ],
 43 |   templateUrl: './review.component.html',
 44 |   styleUrls: ['./review.component.css']
 45 | })
 46 | export class ReviewComponent implements OnInit {
 47 |   actionItems: ActionItem[] = [];
 48 |   selectedItems: Set<string> = new Set();
 49 |   isCreatingTickets = false;
 50 |   priorities = ['High', 'Medium', 'Low'];
 51 | 
 52 |   constructor(
 53 |     private stateService: StateService,
 54 |     private apiService: ApiService,
 55 |     private router: Router,
 56 |     private snackBar: MatSnackBar,
 57 |     private dialog: MatDialog
 58 |   ) {}
 59 | 
 60 |   ngOnInit(): void {
 61 |     this.stateService.actionItems$.subscribe(items => {
 62 |       this.actionItems = items;
 63 |       if (items.length === 0) {
 64 |         this.snackBar.open('No action items found. Please upload a transcript first.', 'Close', {
 65 |           duration: 5000
 66 |         });
 67 |       }
 68 |     });
 69 |   }
 70 | 
 71 |   toggleSelection(id: string): void {
 72 |     const item = this.actionItems.find(i => i.id === id);
 73 |     // Don't allow selection of already created items
 74 |     if (item?.jiraTicket) {
 75 |       return;
 76 |     }
 77 |     
 78 |     if (this.selectedItems.has(id)) {
 79 |       this.selectedItems.delete(id);
 80 |     } else {
 81 |       this.selectedItems.add(id);
 82 |     }
 83 |   }
 84 | 
 85 |   toggleSelectAll(): void {
 86 |     const selectableItems = this.actionItems.filter(item => !item.jiraTicket);
 87 |     if (this.selectedItems.size === selectableItems.length) {
 88 |       this.selectedItems.clear();
 89 |     } else {
 90 |       selectableItems.forEach(item => this.selectedItems.add(item.id));
 91 |     }
 92 |   }
 93 | 
 94 |   isSelected(id: string): boolean {
 95 |     return this.selectedItems.has(id);
 96 |   }
 97 | 
 98 |   allSelected(): boolean {
 99 |     const selectableItems = this.actionItems.filter(item => !item.jiraTicket);
100 |     return selectableItems.length > 0 && this.selectedItems.size === selectableItems.length;
101 |   }
102 | 
103 |   someSelected(): boolean {
104 |     const selectableItems = this.actionItems.filter(item => !item.jiraTicket);
105 |     return this.selectedItems.size > 0 && this.selectedItems.size < selectableItems.length;
106 |   }
107 | 
108 |   getCreatedCount(): number {
109 |     return this.actionItems.filter(item => item.jiraTicket).length;
110 |   }
111 | 
112 |   getFailedCount(): number {
113 |     return this.actionItems.filter(item => item.creationError && !item.jiraTicket).length;
114 |   }
115 | 
116 |   getPendingCount(): number {
117 |     return this.actionItems.filter(item => !item.jiraTicket && !item.creationError).length;
118 |   }
119 | 
120 |   clearCreatedItems(): void {
121 |     const createdIds = this.actionItems
122 |       .filter(item => item.jiraTicket)
123 |       .map(item => item.id);
124 |     
125 |     createdIds.forEach(id => this.stateService.removeActionItem(id));
126 |     this.snackBar.open('Removed created items', 'Close', { duration: 2000 });
127 |   }
128 | 
129 |   retryFailed(): void {
130 |     // Select only failed items
131 |     this.selectedItems.clear();
132 |     this.actionItems
133 |       .filter(item => item.creationError && !item.jiraTicket)
134 |       .forEach(item => {
135 |         this.selectedItems.add(item.id);
136 |         // Clear error before retry
137 |         this.stateService.updateActionItem(item.id, { creationError: undefined });
138 |       });
139 |     
140 |     if (this.selectedItems.size > 0) {
141 |       this.createJiraTickets();
142 |     }
143 |   }
144 | 
145 |   updateItem(id: string, field: keyof ActionItem, value: any): void {
146 |     this.stateService.updateActionItem(id, { [field]: value });
147 |   }
148 | 
149 |   removeItem(id: string): void {
150 |     this.stateService.removeActionItem(id);
151 |     this.selectedItems.delete(id);
152 |     this.snackBar.open('Action item removed', 'Close', { duration: 2000 });
153 |   }
154 | 
155 |   addNewItem(): void {
156 |     const newItem: ActionItem = {
157 |       id: `manual-${Date.now()}`,
158 |       title: 'New Action Item',
159 |       description: '',
160 |       assignee: '',
161 |       priority: 'Medium',
162 |       confidenceScore: 100,
163 |       sourceQuote: 'Manually added',
164 |       dueDate: undefined
165 |     };
166 |     this.stateService.addActionItem(newItem);
167 |     this.snackBar.open('New action item added', 'Close', { duration: 2000 });
168 |   }
169 | 
170 |   getConfidenceColor(score: number): string {
171 |     if (score >= 80) return 'success';
172 |     if (score >= 60) return 'warning';
173 |     return 'error';
174 |   }
175 | 
176 |   getConfidenceLabel(score: number): string {
177 |     if (score >= 80) return 'High';
178 |     if (score >= 60) return 'Medium';
179 |     return 'Low';
180 |   }
181 | 
182 |   createJiraTickets(): void {
183 |     const config = this.stateService.getJiraConfig();
184 |     
185 |     if (!config) {
186 |       this.snackBar.open('Please configure Jira settings first', 'Go to Settings', {
187 |         duration: 5000
188 |       }).onAction().subscribe(() => {
189 |         this.router.navigate(['/settings']);
190 |       });
191 |       return;
192 |     }
193 | 
194 |     const itemsToCreate = this.selectedItems.size > 0
195 |       ? this.actionItems.filter(item => this.selectedItems.has(item.id) && !item.jiraTicket)
196 |       : this.actionItems.filter(item => !item.jiraTicket);
197 | 
198 |     if (itemsToCreate.length === 0) {
199 |       this.snackBar.open('No items to create (all already created or none selected)', 'Close', { duration: 3000 });
200 |       return;
201 |     }
202 | 
203 |     this.isCreatingTickets = true;
204 | 
205 |     this.apiService.createJiraTickets(itemsToCreate, config).subscribe({
206 |       next: (response) => {
207 |         this.isCreatingTickets = false;
208 |         
209 |         if (response.success && response.data) {
210 |           const { created, failed } = response.data;
211 |           
212 |           // Update items with creation status
213 |           created.forEach((ticket, index) => {
214 |             // Match by index in itemsToCreate array
215 |             const item = itemsToCreate[index];
216 |             if (item) {
217 |               this.stateService.updateActionItem(item.id, {
218 |                 jiraTicket: {
219 |                   key: ticket.key,
220 |                   url: ticket.url || `https://${config.domain}/browse/${ticket.key}`,
221 |                   createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
222 |                 }
223 |               });
224 |             }
225 |           });
226 | 
227 |           // Update failed items with error
228 |           failed.forEach((failure: any) => {
229 |             const item = this.actionItems.find(i => i.id === failure.item?.id);
230 |             if (item) {
231 |               this.stateService.updateActionItem(item.id, {
232 |                 creationError: failure.error
233 |               });
234 |             }
235 |           });
236 | 
237 |           // Clear selection of created items
238 |           created.forEach(ticket => {
239 |             const item = this.actionItems.find(i => i.jiraTicket?.key === ticket.key);
240 |             if (item) {
241 |               this.selectedItems.delete(item.id);
242 |             }
243 |           });
244 |           
245 |           if (created.length > 0) {
246 |             this.snackBar.open(
247 |               `✅ Created ${created.length} ticket(s)${failed.length > 0 ? `, ❌ ${failed.length} failed` : ''}`,
248 |               'Close',
249 |               { duration: 5000 }
250 |             );
251 |           } else if (failed.length > 0) {
252 |             this.snackBar.open(
253 |               `❌ Failed to create ${failed.length} ticket(s)`,
254 |               'Close',
255 |               { duration: 5000 }
256 |             );
257 |           }
258 |         } else {
259 |           this.snackBar.open(response.error || 'Failed to create tickets', 'Close', {
260 |             duration: 5000
261 |           });
262 |         }
263 |       },
264 |       error: (error) => {
265 |         this.isCreatingTickets = false;
266 |         this.snackBar.open('Failed to create tickets: ' + error.message, 'Close', {
267 |           duration: 5000
268 |         });
269 |       }
270 |     });
271 |   }
272 | 
273 |   exportToCSV(): void {
274 |     const headers = ['Title', 'Description', 'Assignee', 'Priority', 'Due Date', 'Confidence', 'Source'];
275 |     const rows = this.actionItems.map(item => [
276 |       item.title,
277 |       item.description,
278 |       item.assignee,
279 |       item.priority,
280 |       item.dueDate || '',
281 |       item.confidenceScore.toString(),
282 |       item.sourceQuote
283 |     ]);
284 | 
285 |     const csv = [headers, ...rows]
286 |       .map(row => row.map(cell => `"${cell.replace(/"/g, '""')}"`).join(','))
287 |       .join('\n');
288 | 
289 |     const blob = new Blob([csv], { type: 'text/csv' });
290 |     const url = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);
291 |     const a = document.createElement('a');
292 |     a.href = url;
293 |     a.download = `action-items-${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}.csv`;
294 |     a.click();
295 |     window.URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
296 | 
297 |     this.snackBar.open('CSV exported successfully', 'Close', { duration: 3000 });
298 |   }
299 | 
300 |   exportToJSON(): void {
301 |     const json = JSON.stringify(this.actionItems, null, 2);
302 |     const blob = new Blob([json], { type: 'application/json' });
303 |     const url = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);
304 |     const a = document.createElement('a');
305 |     a.href = url;
306 |     a.download = `action-items-${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}.json`;
307 |     a.click();
308 |     window.URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
309 | 
310 |     this.snackBar.open('JSON exported successfully', 'Close', { duration: 3000 });
311 |   }
312 | }
313 | 
314 | // Made with Bob
</content>

  1 | <div class="review-container">
  2 |   <div class="header">
  3 |     <h1>
  4 |       <mat-icon>checklist</mat-icon>
  5 |       Review Action Items
  6 |     </h1>
  7 |     <p class="subtitle">Review and edit extracted action items before creating Jira tickets</p>
  8 |   </div>
  9 | 
 10 |   <!-- Empty State -->
 11 |   <mat-card *ngIf="actionItems.length === 0" class="empty-state">
 12 |     <mat-card-content>
 13 |       <mat-icon class="empty-icon">inbox</mat-icon>
 14 |       <h2>No Action Items</h2>
 15 |       <p>Upload a transcript and extract action items to get started.</p>
 16 |       <button mat-raised-button color="primary" routerLink="/upload">
 17 |         <mat-icon>cloud_upload</mat-icon>
 18 |         Upload Transcript
 19 |       </button>
 20 |     </mat-card-content>
 21 |   </mat-card>
 22 | 
 23 |   <!-- Action Items List -->
 24 |   <div *ngIf="actionItems.length > 0">
 25 |     <!-- Toolbar -->
 26 |     <mat-card class="toolbar-card">
 27 |       <mat-card-content>
 28 |         <div class="toolbar">
 29 |           <div class="toolbar-left">
 30 |             <mat-checkbox
 31 |               [checked]="allSelected()"
 32 |               [indeterminate]="someSelected()"
 33 |               (change)="toggleSelectAll()"
 34 |             >
 35 |               Select All ({{ selectedItems.size }} / {{ actionItems.length }})
 36 |             </mat-checkbox>
 37 |           </div>
 38 |           
 39 |           <div class="toolbar-right">
 40 |             <div class="status-summary" *ngIf="getCreatedCount() > 0 || getFailedCount() > 0">
 41 |               <span class="created-count" *ngIf="getCreatedCount() > 0">✅ {{ getCreatedCount() }} created</span>
 42 |               <span class="failed-count" *ngIf="getFailedCount() > 0">❌ {{ getFailedCount() }} failed</span>
 43 |               <span class="pending-count" *ngIf="getPendingCount() > 0">⏳ {{ getPendingCount() }} pending</span>
 44 |             </div>
 45 | 
 46 |             <button mat-button (click)="addNewItem()">
 47 |               <mat-icon>add</mat-icon>
 48 |               Add Item
 49 |             </button>
 50 | 
 51 |             <button mat-button (click)="retryFailed()" *ngIf="getFailedCount() > 0">
 52 |               <mat-icon>refresh</mat-icon>
 53 |               Retry Failed
 54 |             </button>
 55 | 
 56 |             <button mat-button (click)="clearCreatedItems()" *ngIf="getCreatedCount() > 0">
 57 |               <mat-icon>clear</mat-icon>
 58 |               Clear Created
 59 |             </button>
 60 |             
 61 |             <button mat-button (click)="exportToCSV()">
 62 |               <mat-icon>download</mat-icon>
 63 |               Export CSV
 64 |             </button>
 65 |             
 66 |             <button mat-button (click)="exportToJSON()">
 67 |               <mat-icon>code</mat-icon>
 68 |               Export JSON
 69 |             </button>
 70 |             
 71 |             <button
 72 |               mat-raised-button
 73 |               color="primary"
 74 |               [disabled]="isCreatingTickets || getPendingCount() === 0"
 75 |               (click)="createJiraTickets()"
 76 |             >
 77 |               <mat-icon>send</mat-icon>
 78 |               {{ isCreatingTickets ? 'Creating...' : 'Create Jira Tickets' }}
 79 |             </button>
 80 |             
 81 |             <mat-spinner *ngIf="isCreatingTickets" diameter="24" class="inline-spinner"></mat-spinner>
 82 |           </div>
 83 |         </div>
 84 |       </mat-card-content>
 85 |     </mat-card>
 86 | 
 87 |     <!-- Action Items Cards -->
 88 |     <div class="items-grid">
 89 |       <mat-card *ngFor="let item of actionItems"
 90 |                 class="item-card"
 91 |                 [class.selected]="isSelected(item.id)"
 92 |                 [class.created]="item.jiraTicket"
 93 |                 [class.failed]="item.creationError && !item.jiraTicket">
 94 |         <mat-card-header>

 96 |             <mat-checkbox
 97 |               [checked]="isSelected(item.id)"
 98 |               [disabled]="!!item.jiraTicket"

100 |               class="item-checkbox"
101 |             ></mat-checkbox>
102 |             
103 |             <!-- Status Badges -->
104 |             <mat-chip *ngIf="item.jiraTicket" class="status-chip created-chip">
105 |               ✅ Created: {{ item.jiraTicket.key }}
106 |             </mat-chip>
107 |

109 |               ❌ Failed
110 |             </mat-chip>
111 |

113 |               *ngIf="!item.jiraTicket"
114 |               [class]="'confidence-chip ' + getConfidenceColor(item.confidenceScore)"
115 |             >
116 |               {{ getConfidenceLabel(item.confidenceScore) }} ({{ item.confidenceScore }}%)
117 |             </mat-chip>
118 |

120 |               *ngIf="item.jiraTicket"

122 |               color="primary"
123 |               [href]="item.jiraTicket.url"
124 |               target="_blank"
125 |               class="view-button"
126 |               title="View in Jira"
127 |             >
128 |               <mat-icon>open_in_new</mat-icon>
129 |             </a>
130 | 
131 |             <button
132 |               mat-icon-button
133 |               color="warn"
134 |               [disabled]="!!item.jiraTicket"
135 |               (click)="removeItem(item.id)"
136 |               class="remove-button"
137 |             >
138 |               <mat-icon>delete</mat-icon>
139 |             </button>
140 |           </div>
141 |         </mat-card-header>
142 |

144 |           <!-- Error Message -->
145 |           <div *ngIf="item.creationError" class="error-message">
146 |             <mat-icon>error</mat-icon>
147 |             <span>{{ item.creationError }}</span>
148 |           </div>
149 | 
150 |           <!-- Created Info -->

152 |             <mat-icon>check_circle</mat-icon>
153 |             <span>Created on {{ item.jiraTicket.createdAt | date:'short' }}</span>
154 |             <a [href]="item.jiraTicket.url" target="_blank" class="jira-link">

156 |               <mat-icon>open_in_new</mat-icon>
157 |             </a>
158 |           </div>
159 | 
160 |           <!-- Title -->
161 |           <mat-form-field class="full-width">
162 |             <mat-label>Title</mat-label>
163 |             <input
164 |               matInput
165 |               [(ngModel)]="item.title"

167 |               (ngModelChange)="updateItem(item.id, 'title', $event)"
168 |               placeholder="Action item title"
169 |             />
170 |           </mat-form-field>
171 | 
172 |           <!-- Description -->
173 |           <mat-form-field class="full-width">
174 |             <mat-label>Description</mat-label>
175 |             <textarea
176 |               matInput
177 |               [(ngModel)]="item.description"
178 |               [disabled]="!!item.jiraTicket"
179 |               (ngModelChange)="updateItem(item.id, 'description', $event)"
180 |               placeholder="Detailed description"
181 |               rows="3"
182 |             ></textarea>
183 |           </mat-form-field>
184 | 
185 |           <!-- Assignee and Priority Row -->
186 |           <div class="form-row">
187 |             <mat-form-field class="half-width">
188 |               <mat-label>Assignee</mat-label>
189 |               <input
190 |                 matInput
191 |                 [(ngModel)]="item.assignee"
192 |                 [disabled]="!!item.jiraTicket"
193 |                 (ngModelChange)="updateItem(item.id, 'assignee', $event)"
194 |                 placeholder="Assignee name"
195 |               />
196 |             </mat-form-field>
197 | 
198 |             <mat-form-field class="half-width">
199 |               <mat-label>Priority</mat-label>
200 |               <mat-select
201 |                 [(ngModel)]="item.priority"
202 |                 [disabled]="!!item.jiraTicket"
203 |                 (ngModelChange)="updateItem(item.id, 'priority', $event)"
204 |               >
205 |                 <mat-option *ngFor="let priority of priorities" [value]="priority">
206 |                   {{ priority }}
207 |                 </mat-option>
208 |               </mat-select>
209 |             </mat-form-field>
210 |           </div>
211 | 
212 |           <!-- Due Date -->
213 |           <mat-form-field class="full-width">
214 |             <mat-label>Due Date</mat-label>
215 |             <input
216 |               matInput
217 |               [matDatepicker]="picker"
218 |               [(ngModel)]="item.dueDate"
219 |               [disabled]="!!item.jiraTicket"
220 |               (ngModelChange)="updateItem(item.id, 'dueDate', $event)"
221 |               placeholder="Select due date"
222 |             />
223 |             <mat-datepicker-toggle matSuffix [for]="picker"></mat-datepicker-toggle>
224 |             <mat-datepicker #picker></mat-datepicker>

226 |

234 |             <p class="timestamp" *ngIf="item.timestamp">
235 |               <mat-icon>schedule</mat-icon>
236 |               {{ item.timestamp }}
237 |             </p>
238 |           </div>
239 |         </mat-card-content>
240 |       </mat-card>
241 |     </div>
242 |   </div>
243 | </div>

<path>frontend/src/app/components/review/review.component.ts</path>
<content>import { Component, OnInit } from '@angular/core';
import { CommonModule } from '@angular/common';
import { Router, RouterLink } from '@angular/router';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';

import { ActionItem } from '../../models/action-item.model';
import { StateService } from '../../services/state.service';
import { ApiService } from '../../services/api.service';
import { ToastService } from '../../services/toast.service';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-review',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [CommonModule, FormsModule, RouterLink],
  templateUrl: './review.component.html',
  styleUrls: ['./review.component.css']
})
export class ReviewComponent implements OnInit {
  actionItems: ActionItem[] = [];
  selectedItems: Set<string> = new Set();
  isCreatingTickets = false;
  priorities = ['High', 'Medium', 'Low'];
  transcript: any = null;

  constructor(
    private stateService: StateService,
    private apiService: ApiService,
    private router: Router,
    private toastService: ToastService
  ) {}

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.stateService.actionItems$.subscribe(items => {
      this.actionItems = items;
    });
    
    this.transcript = this.stateService.getTranscript();
  }

  get meetingFileName(): string {
    return this.transcript?.metadata?.fileName || 'Meeting Transcript';
  }

  toggleSelection(id: string): void {
    const item = this.actionItems.find(i => i.id === id);
    if (item?.jiraTicket) return;
    
    if (this.selectedItems.has(id)) {
      this.selectedItems.delete(id);
    } else {
      this.selectedItems.add(id);
    }
  }

  toggleSelectAll(): void {
    const selectableItems = this.actionItems.filter(item => !item.jiraTicket);
    if (this.selectedItems.size === selectableItems.length) {
      this.selectedItems.clear();
    } else {
      selectableItems.forEach(item => this.selectedItems.add(item.id));
    }
  }

  isSelected(id: string): boolean {
    return this.selectedItems.has(id);
  }

  allSelected(): boolean {
    const selectableItems = this.actionItems.filter(item => !item.jiraTicket);
    return selectableItems.length > 0 && this.selectedItems.size === selectableItems.length;
  }

  getConfidenceClass(score: number): string {
    if (score >= 80) return 'high';
    if (score >= 70) return 'good';
    return 'low';
  }

  getConfidenceLabel(score: number): string {
    if (score >= 80) return 'High confidence';
    if (score >= 70) return 'Good confidence';
    return '⚠ Low confidence';
  }

  updateItem(id: string, field: keyof ActionItem, value: any): void {
    this.stateService.updateActionItem(id, { [field]: value });
  }

  removeItem(id: string): void {
    this.stateService.removeActionItem(id);
    this.selectedItems.delete(id);
    this.toastService.success('Item Removed', 'Action item has been deleted');
  }

  createJiraTickets(): void {
    const config = this.stateService.getJiraConfig();
    
    if (!config) {
      this.toastService.error('Configuration Required', 'Please configure Jira settings first');
      this.router.navigate(['/setup']);
      return;
    }

    const itemsToCreate = this.selectedItems.size > 0
      ? this.actionItems.filter(item => this.selectedItems.has(item.id) && !item.jiraTicket)
      : this.actionItems.filter(item => !item.jiraTicket);

    if (itemsToCreate.length === 0) {
      this.toastService.warning('No Items Selected', 'Please select items to create');
      return;
    }

    this.isCreatingTickets = true;

    this.apiService.createJiraTickets(itemsToCreate, config).subscribe({
      next: (response) => {
        this.isCreatingTickets = false;
        
        if (response.success && response.data) {
          const { created, failed } = response.data;
          
          created.forEach((ticket, index) => {
            const item = itemsToCreate[index];
            if (item) {
              this.stateService.updateActionItem(item.id, {
                jiraTicket: {
                  key: ticket.key,
                  url: ticket.url || `https://${config.domain}/browse/${ticket.key}`,
                  createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
                }
              });
              
              // Show individual success toast with link
              this.toastService.success(
                `${ticket.key} created successfully`,
                'Ticket has been created in Jira',
                {
                  text: 'View in Jira →',
                  url: ticket.url || `https://${config.domain}/browse/${ticket.key}`
                }
              );
            }
          });

          failed.forEach((failure: any) => {
            const item = this.actionItems.find(i => i.id === failure.item?.id);
            if (item) {
              this.stateService.updateActionItem(item.id, {
                creationError: failure.error
              });
            }
          });

          created.forEach(ticket => {
            const item = this.actionItems.find(i => i.jiraTicket?.key === ticket.key);
            if (item) {
              this.selectedItems.delete(item.id);
            }
          });
          
          if (failed.length > 0) {
            this.toastService.error(
              'Some Tickets Failed',
              `${failed.length} ticket(s) could not be created`
            );
          }
        } else {
          this.toastService.error('Creation Failed', response.error || 'Unknown error');
        }
      },
      error: (error) => {
        this.isCreatingTickets = false;
        this.toastService.error('Creation Failed', error.message);
      }
    });
  }

  exportToCSV(): void {
    const headers = ['Title', 'Description', 'Assignee', 'Priority', 'Due Date', 'Confidence', 'Source'];
    const rows = this.actionItems.map(item => [
      item.title,
      item.description,
      item.assignee,
      item.priority,
      item.dueDate || '',
      item.confidenceScore.toString(),
      item.sourceQuote
    ]);

    const csv = [headers, ...rows]
      .map(row => row.map(cell => `"${cell.replace(/"/g, '""')}"`).join(','))
      .join('\n');

    const blob = new Blob([csv], { type: 'text/csv' });
    const url = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = `action-items-${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}.csv`;
    a.click();
    window.URL.revokeObjectURL(url);

    this.toastService.success('Export Complete', 'CSV file has been downloaded');
  }

  exportToJSON(): void {
    const json = JSON.stringify(this.actionItems, null, 2);
    const blob = new Blob([json], { type: 'application/json' });
    const url = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = `action-items-${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}.json`;
    a.click();
    window.URL.revokeObjectURL(url);

    this.toastService.success('Export Complete', 'JSON file has been downloaded');
  }
}

// Made with Bob
</content>
