<path>frontend/src/app/components/review/review.component.html</path>
<content><div class="min-h-screen bg-dark-bg p-6">
  <div class="max-w-7xl mx-auto">
    
    <!-- Empty State -->
    <div *ngIf="actionItems.length === 0" class="flex items-center justify-center min-h-[60vh]">
      <div class="text-center">
        <svg class="w-24 h-24 mx-auto mb-6 text-gray-600" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
          <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M20 13V6a2 2 0 00-2-2H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v7m16 0v5a2 2 0 01-2 2H6a2 2 0 01-2-2v-5m16 0h-2.586a1 1 0 00-.707.293l-2.414 2.414a1 1 0 01-.707.293h-3.172a1 1 0 01-.707-.293l-2.414-2.414A1 1 0 006.586 13H4"></path>
        </svg>
        <h2 class="text-2xl font-bold text-white mb-2">No transcript uploaded yet</h2>
        <p class="text-gray-400 mb-6">Upload a meeting transcript to extract action items</p>
        <a routerLink="/upload" class="inline-flex items-center gap-2 bg-primary hover:bg-blue-600 text-white font-semibold py-3 px-6 rounded-lg transition-colors">
          <svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
            <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M7 16a4 4 0 01-.88-7.903A5 5 0 1115.9 6L16 6a5 5 0 011 9.9M15 13l-3-3m0 0l-3 3m3-3v12"></path>
          </svg>
          Upload Transcript
        </a>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Action Items List -->
    <div *ngIf="actionItems.length > 0">
      <!-- Header -->
      <div class="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between">
        <div>
          <h1 class="text-3xl font-bold text-white mb-2">Review Action Items</h1>
          <div class="flex items-center gap-4 text-sm">
            <span class="px-3 py-1 bg-primary/20 text-primary rounded-full font-medium">
              {{ actionItems.length }} items found
            </span>
            <span class="text-gray-400">{{ meetingFileName }}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Action Bar -->
      <div class="bg-dark-card rounded-xl border border-dark-border p-4 mb-6">
        <div class="flex items-center justify-between flex-wrap gap-4">
          <div class="flex items-center gap-4">
            <label class="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer">
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                [checked]="allSelected()"
                (change)="toggleSelectAll()"
                class="w-5 h-5 rounded border-dark-border bg-dark-bg text-primary focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary focus:ring-offset-0"
              />
              <span class="text-white font-medium">Select All</span>
            </label>
          </div>
          
          <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
            <button
              (click)="exportToCSV()"
              class="px-4 py-2 border border-dark-border hover:border-gray-500 text-gray-400 hover:text-white rounded-lg transition-colors text-sm font-medium"
            >
              Export CSV
            </button>
            
            <button
              (click)="exportToJSON()"
              class="px-4 py-2 border border-dark-border hover:border-gray-500 text-gray-400 hover:text-white rounded-lg transition-colors text-sm font-medium"
            >
              Export JSON
            </button>
            
            <button
              (click)="createJiraTickets()"
              [disabled]="isCreatingTickets || selectedItems.size === 0"
              class="px-6 py-2 bg-primary hover:bg-blue-600 disabled:bg-gray-600 disabled:cursor-not-allowed text-white font-semibold rounded-lg transition-colors flex items-center gap-2"
            >
              <svg *ngIf="isCreatingTickets" class="animate-spin h-5 w-5" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                <circle class="opacity-25" cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="4"></circle>
                <path class="opacity-75" fill="currentColor" d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8V0C5.373 0 0 5.373 0 12h4zm2 5.291A7.962 7.962 0 014 12H0c0 3.042 1.135 5.824 3 7.938l3-2.647z"></path>
              </svg>
              <span>{{ isCreatingTickets ? 'Creating...' : `Create ${selectedItems.size} Jira Ticket${selectedItems.size !== 1 ? 's' : ''}` }}</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Items Grid -->
      <div class="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
        <div
          *ngFor="let item of actionItems"
          class="bg-dark-card rounded-2xl border transition-all"
          [class.border-primary]="isSelected(item.id) && !item.jiraTicket"
          [class.border-success]="item.jiraTicket"
          [class.border-dark-border]="!isSelected(item.id) && !item.jiraTicket"
          [class.border-warning]="item.confidenceScore < 70 && !item.jiraTicket"
          [class.border-dashed]="item.confidenceScore < 70 && !item.jiraTicket"
        >
          <!-- Card Header -->
          <div class="p-6 border-b border-dark-border">
            <div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-4 mb-4">
              <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
                <input
                  *ngIf="!item.jiraTicket"
                  type="checkbox"
                  [checked]="isSelected(item.id)"
                  (change)="toggleSelection(item.id)"
                  class="w-5 h-5 rounded border-dark-border bg-dark-bg text-primary focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary focus:ring-offset-0"
                />
                
                <!-- Priority Badge -->
                <span
                  class="px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold"
                  [class.bg-red-500/20]="item.priority === 'High'"
                  [class.text-red-400]="item.priority === 'High'"
                  [class.bg-warning/20]="item.priority === 'Medium'"
                  [class.text-warning]="item.priority === 'Medium'"
                  [class.bg-blue-500/20]="item.priority === 'Low'"
                  [class.text-blue-400]="item.priority === 'Low'"
                >
                  {{ item.priority }}
                </span>
                
                <!-- Confidence Badge -->
                <span
                  *ngIf="!item.jiraTicket"
                  class="px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold"
                  [class.bg-success/20]="item.confidenceScore >= 80"
                  [class.text-success]="item.confidenceScore >= 80"
                  [class.bg-primary/20]="item.confidenceScore >= 70 && item.confidenceScore < 80"
                  [class.text-primary]="item.confidenceScore >= 70 && item.confidenceScore < 80"
                  [class.bg-warning/20]="item.confidenceScore < 70"
                  [class.text-warning]="item.confidenceScore < 70"
                >
                  {{ getConfidenceLabel(item.confidenceScore) }}
                </span>
              </div>
              
              <button
                *ngIf="!item.jiraTicket"
                (click)="removeItem(item.id)"
                class="text-gray-400 hover:text-red-400 transition-colors"
              >
                <svg class="w-5 h-5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                  <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M19 7l-.867 12.142A2 2 0 0116.138 21H7.862a2 2 0 01-1.995-1.858L5 7m5 4v6m4-6v6m1-10V4a1 1 0 00-1-1h-4a1 1 0 00-1 1v3M4 7h16"></path>
                </svg>
              </button>
            </div>

            <!-- Low Confidence Warning -->
            <div *ngIf="item.confidenceScore < 70 && !item.jiraTicket" class="bg-warning/10 border border-warning/30 rounded-lg p-3 mb-4">
              <div class="flex items-start gap-2">
                <svg class="w-5 h-5 text-warning flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                  <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M12 9v2m0 4h.01m-6.938 4h13.856c1.54 0 2.502-1.667 1.732-3L13.732 4c-.77-1.333-2.694-1.333-3.464 0L3.34 16c-.77 1.333.192 3 1.732 3z"></path>
                </svg>
                <div>
                  <p class="text-warning font-semibold text-sm">Low Confidence</p>
                  <p class="text-gray-400 text-xs mt-1">AI wasn't fully certain about this item. Please verify before pushing to Jira.</p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <!-- Jira Ticket Badge -->
            <div *ngIf="item.jiraTicket" class="bg-success/10 border border-success rounded-lg p-3 mb-4">
              <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
                <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <svg class="w-5 h-5 text-success" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                    <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M9 12l2 2 4-4m6 2a9 9 0 11-18 0 9 9 0 0118 0z"></path>
                  </svg>
                  <span class="text-success font-semibold">{{ item.jiraTicket.key }}</span>
                </div>
                <a
                  [href]="item.jiraTicket.url"
                  target="_blank"
                  class="text-primary hover:text-blue-400 text-sm font-medium flex items-center gap-1"
                >
                  View in Jira
                  <svg class="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                    <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M10 6H6a2 2 0 00-2 2v10a2 2 0 002 2h10a2 2 0 002-2v-4M14 4h6m0 0v6m0-6L10 14"></path>
                  </svg>
                </a>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- Card Body -->
          <div class="p-6 space-y-4">
            <!-- Title -->
            <div>
              <label class="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-400 mb-2">Title</label>
              <input
                type="text"
                [(ngModel)]="item.title"
                [disabled]="!!item.jiraTicket"
                (ngModelChange)="updateItem(item.id, 'title', $event)"
                class="w-full bg-dark-bg border border-dark-border rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-white placeholder-gray-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary focus:border-transparent disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
              />
            </div>

            <!-- Description -->
            <div>
              <label class="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-400 mb-2">Description</label>
              <textarea
                [(ngModel)]="item.description"
                [disabled]="!!item.jiraTicket"
                (ngModelChange)="updateItem(item.id, 'description', $event)"
                rows="3"
                class="w-full bg-dark-bg border border-dark-border rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-white placeholder-gray-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary focus:border-transparent disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed resize-none"
              ></textarea>
            </div>

            <!-- Assignee and Priority -->
            <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div>
                <label class="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-400 mb-2">Assignee</label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  [(ngModel)]="item.assignee"
                  [disabled]="!!item.jiraTicket"
                  (ngModelChange)="updateItem(item.id, 'assignee', $event)"
                  placeholder="Unassigned"
                  class="w-full bg-dark-bg border border-dark-border rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-white placeholder-gray-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary focus:border-transparent disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
                />
              </div>
              
              <div>
                <label class="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-400 mb-2">Priority</label>
                <select
                  [(ngModel)]="item.priority"
                  [disabled]="!!item.jiraTicket"
                  (ngModelChange)="updateItem(item.id, 'priority', $event)"
                  class="w-full bg-dark-bg border border-dark-border rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary focus:border-transparent disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
                >
                  <option *ngFor="let priority of priorities" [value]="priority">{{ priority }}</option>
                </select>
              </div>
            </div>

            <!-- Due Date -->
            <div>
              <label class="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-400 mb-2">Due Date</label>
              <input
                type="date"
                [(ngModel)]="item.dueDate"
                [disabled]="!!item.jiraTicket"
                (ngModelChange)="updateItem(item.id, 'dueDate', $event)"
                class="w-full bg-dark-bg border border-dark-border rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary focus:border-transparent disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
              />
            </div>

            <!-- Source Quote -->
            <div class="border-l-4 border-primary bg-primary/5 rounded-r-lg p-4">
              <div class="flex items-center gap-2 text-gray-400 text-xs mb-2">
                <svg class="w-4 h-4" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                  <path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M7 8h10M7 12h4m1 8l-4-4H5a2 2 0 01-2-2V6a2 2 0 012-2h14a2 2 0 012 2v8a2 2 0 01-2 2h-3l-4 4z"></path>
                </svg>
                <span>Source Quote</span>
              </div>
              <p class="text-gray-300 text-sm italic">{{ item.sourceQuote }}</p>
              <p *ngIf="item.timestamp" class="text-gray-500 text-xs mt-2">{{ item.timestamp }}</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- Made with Bob -->
</content>
